Evidence: `CHK-DMA-INTR-RELEASE PASS seed=<n> kind=<k> intr_dma_error_after_w1c=0 src11_after_w1c=0 control_intr_dma_error_before_w1c=1 intr_state_before=0x<b> intr_state_after=0x<a>`. The release of INTR_STATE.DMA_DONE and DMA_CHUNK_DONE is not graded here.
|CHK-DMA-HI-ADDR
|A copy with legal LO addresses, ADDR_SPACE_ID 0x77 and a non-zero SRC_ADDR_HI or DST_ADDR_HI moves no data to the 32-bit destination: every word of the destination at DST_ADDR_LO and of its two guards reads back the sentinel, both when the copy ends and again after the recovery writes, before the sentinel refill. The copy ends with DONE or ERROR within the bounded wait, and the recovery copy with both ADDR_HI registers 0 then completes with DONE=1, ERROR=0 and the destination equal to the source (`dma.adoc`, SEP secure DMA integration: 32-bit master addresses, ASID 0x7 the only route). A DUT that drops ADDR_HI and copies to the low 32 bits changes the sentinel. STATUS.ERROR and ERROR_CODE are logged only, because no document defines the ADDR_HI rule for the SEP DMA route: `OBS-DMA-HI-ADDR seed=<n> side=<src\|dst> addr_hi=0x<h> error=<e> done=<d> src_err=<a> dst_err=<b> error_code=0x<c> STATUS=0x<s> ...`. Anchored on the frontdoor read-back of the SRAM destination. Control: the same copy with both ADDR_HI registers 0 completes with the source data in the same seed (`CTL-DMA-HI-ADDR LOG seed=<n> ... control_ok=1`), so a DMA that moves no data at all fails the control. Evidence: `CHK-DMA-HI-ADDR PASS seed=<n> side=<src\|dst> addr_hi=0x<h> lo_dst=0x<a> size=<s> ended=1 done=<d> footprint_words=<w> footprint_changed=0 footprint_changed_late=0 recovery_done=1 control_ok=1 recovery_mismatch=0 recovery_guard_bad=0`.
|CHK-DMA-LOCK
|After the write of MuBi4 False 0x9 to RANGE_REGWEN, and after each later RANGE_REGWEN write of 0x6, 0xF, 0x9 and 0x0, RANGE_REGWEN reads 0x9, BASE and LIMIT read the values written before the lock and RANGE_VALID reads 1 (`sep-programming.adoc`, zero fill procedure: 0x9 fixes the window until reset; `dma.adoc`, Zero Fill; `dma.hjson`, RANGE_VALID: regwen RANGE_REGWEN). The expected value is the value written before the lock, not a read-back taken after it, and every value written after the lock differs from it. A DUT that ignores the lock write or lets a write re-open it reads RANGE_REGWEN other than 0x9 and a changed BASE or LIMIT, and a DUT that leaves RANGE_VALID writable reads 0. Anchored on frontdoor read-back after each attempt, from idle and after a size error. Control (`CTL-DMA-LOCK`, `CTRL-MISSING` if it fails): before the lock the BASE, LIMIT and RANGE_VALID writes landed and RANGE_REGWEN read 0x6; in the error context STATUS.ERROR rose before the lock. Evidence: `CHK-DMA-LOCK PASS seed=<n> ctx=<idle\|after_error> attempt=<lock\|0x<v>> regwen=0x9/0x9 base=0x<got>/0x<pre_lock> limit=0x<got>/0x<pre_lock> valid=<got>/1 new=0x<b>/0x<l>/0 write_resp=<r> control_pre_lock_landed=1`, ten lines per seed. The line `OBS-DMA-RANGE-OUT seed=<n> side=<src\|dst> dir=<below\|above> dist=<d> error=<e> src_err=<a> dst_err=<b>` records the logged-only cells.
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every named checker PASSes. UVM_ERROR == 0.

*Scope limit*: The open interrupt tests keep INTR_TEST routes and the DMA_DONE interrupt. `sep_dma_basic_test` keeps the opcode, address and ASID error legs, the width encoding 3, one lock and one rejected write. Not claimed: the release of INTR_STATE.DMA_DONE and DMA_CHUNK_DONE by a write of 1 to STATUS, the release of a bit that INTR_TEST forced, INTR_STATE with the enable clear, the response to a region outside the range, the STATUS and ERROR_CODE response to a non-zero ADDR_HI (logged only), a region that straddles an edge, the lock while the engine is BUSY, DONE, BUSY and the beats after an error, and the same-cycle fault and clear race of `DMA_BUS_ERR_CLEAR` (see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>). The release of the lock by a cold reset and the reset values of the range registers are graded in entry 5.15. The data transfer contract is graded in entry 4.12.

'''''

[[sep_dma_abort_recovery_rand_test]]
===== 4.14 `sep_dma_abort_recovery_rand_test`

*Phase*: 3

*Area*: SPI and DMA

*Run mode*: `no_cpu` (`lsu_stub_all_live`) with real fuse sense (no `+skip_fuse_sense`), because the leaf asserts `rst_ni`. 4 seeds. Verilator and VCS; testlist groups `cpu_stub` and `all`, file `system.toml`, one testlist entry. Two abort runs per seed, each from a cold reset.

*Source Module*: `sep_dma_abort_recovery_rand_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip register frontdoor over `s_axi` (also for the SRAM source and the destination image). Approved probes: `dma_busy_probe_o` and `dbg_sep_reset_n_o`. `dma_axi_req_probe_o` and `dma_status_probe_o` (checker CHK-DMA-ABORT-BEATS, and the phase class of each run) are observation taps the test adds to the SEP_TB_ARCH observation-probe list. No inject.

*Randomization*: `SepSeededRng` from the run seed. Dimensions and legal ranges: chunk size C (1024 to 4096 bytes, a multiple of 4), chunk count n (from the larger of 2 and ceil(4096 / C) to floor(16384 / C)) and transfer size T = n * C (4 KB to 16 KB), so that n is 2 or more and every chunk holds at least 256 transactions; the phase class of abort run 2 (first transaction, chunk boundary or final transaction); abort point inside the class; source and destination offsets (footprints with guards disjoint and inside BASE to LIMIT); all data. Run 1 of every seed has the class mid chunk. Source and destination offsets are multiples of 4, T and C are multiples of 4, and T is a multiple of C (`secure_dma.rdl`, SRC_ADDR_LO, DST_ADDR_LO, TRANSFER_WIDTH). The delay d counts clocks from the end of the first GO write to the start of the ABORT write. The reference copy measures its clean transfer time t_clean on the same base: the clocks from the end of its first GO write to the clock at which its STATUS poll first reads DONE=1, with the gaps of the re-GO writes included. The class gives d as a fraction of t_clean: first transaction, the first delay of the sequence 0, s, 2s, ... with s = t_clean / (10 n) that gives busy_at_abort = 1; mid chunk, 25 to 75 percent of the share t_clean / n of the first chunk; chunk boundary, within 5 percent of j * t_clean / n for a drawn j from 1 to n - 1; final transaction, 90 to 99 percent of t_clean. The value t_clean is measured and sets the stimulus only. It is never an expected value. `reseed = 4` samples four different sizes, run-2 classes, points, offsets and data sets. The class of run 2 depends on the seed, so the three classes other than mid chunk close on a merge of regressions (<<sep-fcov-rand,SEP_FCOV>>).

*Objective*: The DMA abort contract holds (`secure_dma.adoc`, CONTROL.ABORT, STATUS.ABORTED). Writing CONTROL.ABORT during a transfer sets STATUS.ABORTED once the aborted operation drains, so no DMA beat (AR, AW, W or B handshake) follows the ABORTED rise. ABORTED clears by write-one-to-clear. A copy after recovery completes with the right data. The open DMA tests `sep_dma_basic_test`, `sep_dma_hash_test` and `sep_dma_cpu_contention_test` do not grade CONTROL.ABORT or STATUS.ABORTED.

*Test Coverage*: ABORT in the middle of a chunk of a 4 KB to 16 KB copy on every seed, and at the first transaction, at a chunk boundary or at the final transaction in a second run whose class the seed draws (these three classes close on a merge of regressions), the rise of ABORTED, the write-one-to-clear of ABORTED, the stability of the destination image after ABORTED, and a recovery copy after the abort with no reset. The data written on the SoC side is not graded, because the CONTROL.ABORT text gives no guarantee there. The test builds on the open DMA tests named in the objective, which grade no abort cell. Functional coverage: `sep_dma_error_terminal_cg` (`cp_abort_outcome`, `cp_abort_quiet`), `sep_dma_xfer_rand_cg` (`cp_abort_phase`, graded on a merge of regressions), `sep_dma_range_config_cg` (`cp_range_valid_at_go`) and `sep_dma_endpoint_cg` (`cp_src_x_dst_endpoint`).

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Draw every value from the seed and log it in `DRAW` lines. Every run below except the recovery starts with a cold reset: assert `rst_ni`, release it, and wait with a bound for the `sep_reset_n` release on `dbg_sep_reset_n_o` before the first register access.
. Reference copy. After a cold reset write BASE = 0x10000000, LIMIT = 0x1003FFFF (the SEP SRAM, `memory_map.adoc`) and RANGE_VALID = 1. Write ADDR_SPACE_ID = 0x77 and both ADDR_HI registers = 0. Fill the source with unique words and the destination and the guards with sentinel words. Write the addresses, TOTAL_DATA_SIZE = T, CHUNK_DATA_SIZE = C, TRANSFER_WIDTH = 2, INCREMENT=1 and WRAP=0 on both sides. Write CONTROL with OPCODE 0, INITIAL_TRANSFER=1 and GO=1.
. Reference copy. Poll STATUS with a bounded wait until DONE=1 and BUSY=0. A timeout fails with `FAIL-DMA-TIMEOUT`. When STATUS reads CHUNK_DONE=1, DONE=0 and BUSY=0, write 1 to STATUS.CHUNK_DONE, write CONTROL with OPCODE 0, GO=1 and INITIAL_TRANSFER=0, and wait with a bound until `dma_busy_probe_o` has risen since that write before the next evaluation (the re-GO rule). Compare the destination with the source. If it differs, fail with CTRL-MISSING. Measure t_clean in clocks from the end of the first GO write to the clock at which this poll first reads DONE=1.
. Abort run. For each of the two runs (run 1 has the class mid chunk; run 2 has the drawn class): run a cold reset. Program the range and the same configuration as the reference copy. Fill the source and write sentinel words to the destination and guards. Read STATUS and log ABORTED (the control of CHK-DMA-ABORT).
. Abort run. Write CONTROL with OPCODE 0, INITIAL_TRANSFER=1 and GO=1. Sample `dma_busy_probe_o` at every clock from the GO write and record whether it has risen. While the delay d runs, poll STATUS and apply the re-GO rule of step 3 to each read of CHUNK_DONE=1, DONE=0 and BUSY=0.
. Abort run. Error injection. After the delay d of the run, write CONTROL with ABORT=1, OPCODE 0, GO=0 and INITIAL_TRANSFER=0, so the write requests no new operation (`secure_dma.adoc`, CONTROL.GO: setting this bit triggers the DMA operation) (point: the CONTROL register during a transfer; kind: abort of an active transfer, in the phase class of the run). Expected response (`secure_dma.adoc`, CONTROL.ABORT, STATUS.ABORTED): ABORTED reads 1 and no further beat follows. Sample `dma_busy_probe_o` on the clock in which the ABORT write completes on the bus (`busy_at_abort`). The data written on the SoC side is not graded, because the CONTROL.ABORT text gives no guarantee there.
. Abort run. If `busy_at_abort` is 0, the abort did not arrive during a transfer. The leaf logs `OBS-DMA-ABORT-REDRAW`, grades nothing from the run, and repeats the abort run from a cold reset with a changed delay, at most 8 times for the run. When the probe has not risen since the GO write, the engine has not started (case `not_started`): use a larger delay. When the probe has risen, the transfer has ended or is between chunks (case `ended`): use a smaller delay, never below 0. Each change is t_clean / (10 n). If no repeat reaches `busy_at_abort` = 1, the leaf fails with CTRL-MISSING.
. Abort run. If `busy_at_abort` is 1, poll STATUS until ABORTED=1 with a bounded wait. A timeout fails CHK-DMA-ABORT. In the final-transaction class, when the bounded wait ends with DONE=1 and ABORTED=0, the transfer finished before the abort took effect. In the chunk-boundary class, when the bounded wait ends with CHUNK_DONE=1 or DONE=1, BUSY=0 and ABORTED=0, the chunk finished before the abort took effect. In both cases the leaf logs `OBS-DMA-ABORT-REDRAW` (case `finished`), grades nothing from the run, and repeats the abort run with a smaller delay within the 8 repeats of step 7. When ABORTED reads 1, read STATUS and CONTROL and log BUSY, GO, DONE and ERROR (`OBS-DMA-ABORT`).
. Abort run. Read the destination footprint and the guards (image 1). Wait as long as t_clean. Read them again (image 2). Compare image 1 with image 2 (CHK-DMA-ABORT-QUIET). Count the destination words that differ from the sentinel and the words that equal it.
. Abort run. Read STATUS (ABORTED reads 1). Write 1 to STATUS.ABORTED only. Read STATUS (CHK-DMA-ABORT-CLR).
. Recovery with no reset. Run the DMA clean state. Write CONTROL with GO=0, because the GO state after an abort is not stated. Keep the range registers and program a new copy of drawn size with fresh source words and sentinel destination and guards (the addresses, TOTAL_DATA_SIZE, CHUNK_DATA_SIZE, TRANSFER_WIDTH = 2, INCREMENT=1 and WRAP=0 on both sides). Write CONTROL with OPCODE 0, INITIAL_TRANSFER=1 and GO=1. Poll DONE with a bounded wait, with the re-GO rule of step 3. Read ERROR, the destination and the guards (CHK-DMA-RECOVER).
. Seed control. Check that at least one graded abort run of the seed shows both a destination word that differs from the sentinel and a destination word that equals it. Run 1 of the seed has the class mid chunk for this reason. If no run shows both, fail with CTRL-MISSING.
. Beat checks. Sample `dma_axi_req_probe_o` and `dma_status_probe_o` at every clock from the GO write of each graded run to the DONE of its recovery copy, and grade CHK-DMA-ABORT-BEATS. The leaf also compares the beat count at the ABORT write with the phase class of the run; a run outside its class is repeated from a cold reset with a changed delay, up to a bound that is a test parameter, and at the bound the leaf fails with CTRL-MISSING. So each run lands in its class.

*Checkers*:

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|CHK-DMA-ABORT
|In a run with `dma_busy_probe_o` = 1 when the ABORT write completes on the bus, ABORTED reads 1 within the bounded wait (`secure_dma.adoc`, STATUS.ABORTED: set once the aborted operation drains). A DUT that ignores ABORT never sets the bit and times out. Anchored on the BUSY probe and STATUS readback. Control: ABORTED reads 0 before the GO write, so a bit left set cannot pass. A run with `busy_at_abort` = 0 is repeated and not graded. In the final-transaction class a run whose bounded wait ends with DONE=1 and ABORTED=0, and in the chunk-boundary class a run whose bounded wait ends with CHUNK_DONE=1 or DONE=1, BUSY=0 and ABORTED=0, is also repeated and not graded, because the specification gives no rule for an abort that arrives in the last transaction of a chunk or of the transfer. In the first-transaction and mid-chunk classes that outcome fails the check, so a DUT that ignores ABORT fails in the mid-chunk run that every seed grades. Evidence: `CHK-DMA-ABORT PASS seed=<n> phase=<p> busy_at_abort=1 aborted=1 aborted_before=0 dest_words_written=<w> redraws=<r>`. The lines `OBS-DMA-ABORT-REDRAW seed=<n> phase=<p> busy_at_abort=<b> case=<not_started\|ended\|finished>` and `OBS-DMA-ABORT seed=<n> busy=<b> go=<g> done=<d> error=<e>` record the repeated runs and the values that the register description does not state.
|CHK-DMA-ABORT-QUIET
|The destination image read after ABORTED is seen equals the destination image read again after a wait as long as t_clean (`secure_dma.adoc`, CONTROL.ABORT, STATUS.ABORTED). A DUT that keeps writing after ABORTED writes a source word over a sentinel word, and every source byte differs from the sentinel byte s, so the second image differs from the first. Anchored on frontdoor reads of the destination. Control: in the seed at least one run left both a written word and a sentinel word, so work remained when the abort arrived (the seed control of step 12). Evidence: `CHK-DMA-ABORT-QUIET PASS seed=<n> image_after_aborted_eq_image_later=1 control_nonsentinel_words=<w> control_sentinel_words=<s>`.
|CHK-DMA-ABORT-BEATS
|No DMA beat (AR, AW, W or B handshake) appears from the rise of STATUS.ABORTED to the GO write of the recovery copy of step 11. ABORTED sets once the aborted operation drains (`secure_dma.adoc`, STATUS.ABORTED), so the write data and the write responses of the aborted operation complete before the rise. The window holds no reset and no write of GO=1 (the ABORT write of step 6 carries GO=0), so a compliant DUT issues no beat in it. A DUT that issues a request after the rise, or that sets ABORTED before a W beat or a B response of the aborted operation completes, shows the beat on the probe. Anchored on the ABORTED level of `dma_status_probe_o` and the AR, AW, W and B handshakes of `dma_axi_req_probe_o`. The probe carries no R channel, so the check does not grade R beats. Controls: one or more AR or AW beats appear between the GO write and the ABORT write, and one or more beats on each of the AR, AW, W and B channels appear after the recovery GO write, so each graded channel of the probe shows beats in the same run. Evidence: `CHK-DMA-ABORT-BEATS PASS seed=<n> phase=<p> beats_before_abort=<k> beats_after_aborted=0 beats_recovery=<m> ... ar_recovery=<a> aw_recovery=<b> w_recovery=<c> b_recovery=<d> ar_after=0 aw_after=0 w_after=0 b_after=0` with k, a, b, c and d each 1 or more.
|CHK-DMA-ABORT-CLR
|A write of 1 to STATUS.ABORTED clears it (`secure_dma.adoc`, STATUS.ABORTED: RW1C). A DUT with a stuck bit still reads 1. Anchored on frontdoor STATUS readback. Control: ABORTED reads 1 on the read just before the write. Evidence: `CHK-DMA-ABORT-CLR PASS seed=<n> aborted_before_w1c=1 aborted_after_w1c=0`.
|CHK-DMA-RECOVER
|A recovery copy after the abort, with no reset between the abort and the copy, sets DONE, leaves ERROR clear, copies the data and leaves the guards intact (`secure_dma.adoc`, STATUS.DONE, STATUS.ERROR; `sep-programming.adoc`, DMA: wait for STATUS.ABORTED before reusing the buffers or the configuration). A DUT that stays wedged after the abort shows no DONE, an error or a wrong destination. Anchored on frontdoor reads of STATUS, ERROR_CODE and the destination. Evidence: `CHK-DMA-RECOVER PASS seed=<n> reset_between=0 done=1 error=0 mem_mismatch=0 guard_ok=1`.
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every named checker PASSes. UVM_ERROR == 0.

*Scope limit*: Not claimed: ABORT with an idle engine, BUSY, DONE, ERROR and GO after the abort (see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>), and the destination content after the abort. In the final-transaction class a run that ends with DONE=1 and ABORTED=0, and in the chunk-boundary class a run that ends with CHUNK_DONE=1 or DONE=1 and ABORTED=0, is repeated and not graded, so an abort that arrives in the last transaction of a chunk or of the transfer is not claimed. The abort phase class is the aim of the delay. The checks do not depend on the class that the run hit. The open DMA tests keep the copy contract that the recovery copy uses as a control.

'''''

[[sep_spi_dma_handshake_rand_test]]
===== 4.15 `sep_spi_dma_handshake_rand_test`

*Phase*: 3

*Area*: SPI and DMA

*Run mode*: `no_cpu` (`lsu_stub_all_live`) with real fuse sense (no `+skip_fuse_sense`), because the leaf asserts `rst_ni`; `+sep_spi_four_lane`. 3 seeds; tool: both (Verilator and VCS); testlist groups `cpu_stub` and `all`, file `spi.toml`.

*Source Module*: `sep_spi_dma_handshake_rand_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip register frontdoor over `s_axi` (also for the destination image); the SPI responder `OcahSpiFlash` bound to all four data lanes (`quad_io` mode, `dq_out` = `spi_sd_o`, `dq_in` = `spi_sd_i`; the leaf fails at start without `+sep_spi_four_lane`), serving the READ (0x03) of the handshake runs with unique words at address 0 and the lane reads with seeded data at three seeded addresses. `OcahSpiMonitor` (`ocah_spi_vip`, through `hw/sys/sep/dv/cocotb/env/sep_spi_wire_monitor.py`, with the output-enable taps) runs one session per run with SPI traffic: it starts after the `rst_ni` release and stops, with the bus idle, before the next `rst_ni` pulse; each session starts with an empty flash record and an empty monitor history and has its own `SepSpiScoreboard` (`hw/sys/sep/dv/cocotb/env/sep_spi_scoreboard.py`). Approved probes: `spi_lsio_trigger_probe_o`, `dma_busy_probe_o`, `spi_tx_qd_probe_o`, `dbg_sep_reset_n_o`, and bit 9 of `sep_internal_interrupts_probe_o` (PIC source 10, DMA chunk done, `interrupts.adoc`) for CHK-HS-NOCHUNK and its control. The probe `spi_tx_qd_probe_o` is sampled and logged only. The probe `dma_busy_probe_o` is sampled and logged, and it orders the re-GO of the normal-mode control. No added tap. No inject. No check is blocked.

*Randomization*: `SepSeededRng` from the run seed. Dimensions and legal ranges: chunk size C (4 to 16 bytes, multiple of 4); chunk count n (2 to 8); HANDSHAKE_INTR_ENABLE.MASK bits 10:1 (any value; those handshake inputs are tied low); stop count n_stop (1 to n - 1); CLKDIV; RX_WATERMARK W (C/4 to 7); the trigger-cell watermark W_eq (1 to 4); the lane-read order, the x2 opcode (0x3B or 0xBB), the x4 opcode (0x6B or 0xEB), and per lane read a distinct sector, an address and 8 to 32 data bytes; the unique flash words; the sentinel word of the destination, drawn after the reference run of step 4 as a word that equals no word of the reference stream, so that the poll of step 6 ends and the chunk count of step 8 holds for every Rx word that the host returns. The RX FIFO depth must exceed W + 1, else the leaf fails with CTRL-MISSING. `reseed = 3` samples three different chunk shapes, mask bits, stop counts, CLKDIV values, watermarks and word sets.

*Objective*: The DMA hardware handshake contract holds (`dma.adoc`, OCAH Modifications; `secure_dma.adoc`, CHUNK_DATA_SIZE, CONTROL.GO, CONTROL.HARDWARE_HANDSHAKE_ENABLE, STATUS.CHUNK_DONE, HANDSHAKE_INTR_ENABLE; `sep-programming.adoc`, DMA). In hardware handshake mode the DMA reads chunks of CHUNK_DATA_SIZE from the peripheral when the trigger of an enabled handshake input is asserted. The SPI trigger `lsio_trigger_o` is the registered OR of `TXQD < TX_WATERMARK` and `RXQD >= RX_WATERMARK`, and it does not depend on EVENT_ENABLE or INTR_ENABLE (`spi.adoc`, DMA Trigger). The DMA does not clear GO in handshake operation (`secure_dma.adoc`, CONTROL.GO), raises no CHUNK_DONE (CHUNK_DONE is only for memory-to-memory transfers), and stops when firmware clears GO. The trigger rises when RXQD reaches RX_WATERMARK (`RXQD >= RX_WATERMARK`, so at RXQD equal to RX_WATERMARK). Every RX byte packs into RXDATA with `ByteOrder = 1`, and a read at x2 or x4 follows the lane contract of the Rules of this section (Lane data). The open tests `sep_spi_ot_dma_rx_test` and `sep_spi_ot_dma_tx_test` run one handshake configuration with mask bit 0 set, and `sep_spi_ot_dma_tx_test` grades that the DMA waits for the trigger in the TX direction. Those cells are controls here.

*Test Coverage*: Mask bit 0 set (the RX words of n chunks in order) and clear (the DMA moves no word while the trigger is high), random upper mask bits 10:1, GO still set after n chunks, no CHUNK_DONE in the handshake run, the stop after firmware clears GO, the trigger at RXQD equal to RX_WATERMARK on every seed, and one flash read at each of x1, x2 and x4. The test builds on the open tests `sep_spi_ot_dma_rx_test` and `sep_spi_ot_dma_tx_test`. Only the RX word order over n chunks, the masked negative, GO persistence, the stop after the GO clear, no CHUNK_DONE, random upper mask bits, the trigger equality cell and the lane reads are graded here. The open owners keep the rest. Functional coverage: `sep_dma_completion_cg` (`cp_go_lifecycle`, `cp_chunk_done_class`), `sep_dma_transfer_cg` (`cp_peripheral_stream`), `sep_dma_endpoint_cg` (`cp_src_x_dst_endpoint`), `sep_spi_dma_use_case_cg` (`cp_use_case_matrix`), `sep_spi_flash_cg` (`cp_hs`), `sep_spi_lane_data_cg` (cells `dma_handshake` x x1, x2, x4 x RX) and `sep_spi_lane_trigger_cg` (`cp_rxqd_eq_rx_watermark`).

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Draw C, n, the mask bits 10:1, n_stop, CLKDIV, W, W_eq, the lane reads and the flash words from the seed. Each run with SPI traffic below is one monitor session. Keep C/4 at or below W. Log them in `DRAW` lines. Every run below starts with a cold reset: assert `rst_ni`, release it, and wait with a bound for the `sep_reset_n` release on `dbg_sep_reset_n_o` before the first register access. CFG_REGWEN locks the DMA configuration registers while the DMA operates (`dma.hjson`, CFG_REGWEN) and a handshake run keeps GO set, so each run starts from a cold reset and programs its own configuration.
. SPI sequence (used by every run below). Write CONTROL with SPIEN=1, OUTPUT_EN=1, TX_WATERMARK=0 and RX_WATERMARK=W, so that only the RX term can raise the trigger. Write EVENT_ENABLE=0 and INTR_ENABLE=0. The trigger does not depend on either enable (`spi.adoc`, DMA Trigger), so a DUT whose trigger needs an enable moves no word: the poll of step 6 times out, and the trigger controls of CHK-HS-MASKED and CHK-HS-STOP log CTRL-MISSING. Write CSID=0 and CONFIGOPTS with the drawn CLKDIV. Load the read command words of `OcahSpiFlash` into TXDATA. Write COMMAND for one Tx segment (DIRECTION Tx, SPEED 0, CSAAT=1) that carries the command. Write COMMAND for one Rx segment (DIRECTION Rx, SPEED 0, CSAAT=0) of the length that the run states.
. Depth check. After a cold reset run the SPI sequence with an Rx segment of 4*(W+1) bytes and no DMA and no RXDATA read. Poll STATUS until ACTIVE=0 with a bounded wait. If STATUS.RXFULL reads 1, fail with CTRL-MISSING. The RX depth exceeds W + 1 otherwise. The leaf uses the depth only to size the stimulus.
. Reference run. After a cold reset run the SPI sequence with an Rx segment of n*C + 4*(W+1) bytes. Read RXDATA (`spi_controller.rdl`, RXDATA at offset 0x24 of the SPI base `0x10B00000` in `memory_map.adoc`) through the frontdoor until the segment is done and STATUS shows RXEMPTY=1, n*C/4 + W + 1 words, and poll STATUS.RXEMPTY=0 before each read. These words are the reference stream. The golden of CHK-HS-DATA is the first n*C/4 words of the flash bytes at address 0, packed with `ByteOrder = 1`; the reference stream is a logged control (`CTL-HS-REF LOG seed=<n> words=<k> ref_distinct=<0|1> ref_match_golden=<m>/<n*C/4> ...`). Every later run sends the same read command to the same flash content after a cold reset with the same SPI configuration, so every Rx word of a later run is a word of the reference stream. Draw the sentinel word from the seed as a word that equals no word of the reference stream, and log it. Log whether the n*C/4 reference words are pairwise distinct (`ref_distinct`, the control of CHK-HS-DATA, because a reference with a repeated word cannot show a duplicate or a missing word).
. Trigger cell. After a cold reset run the SPI sequence with RX_WATERMARK = W_eq and an Rx segment of 4*(W_eq-1) bytes (none when W_eq is 1), wait until it is done, read STATUS (RXQD = W_eq-1) and the trigger probe. Then run an Rx segment of 4 bytes and wait until it is done; take the clock of the trigger rise and of the last sample edge of the segment, read STATUS (RXQD = W_eq) and the trigger probe. Drain the words.
. Lane reads. After a cold reset, write CONTROL, EVENT_ENABLE, INTR_ENABLE, CSID and CONFIGOPTS as the SPI sequence does (the `rst_ni` pulse clears the host configuration). Then, for each width in the drawn order, run one flash read: the opcode (and for 0x03, 0x3B and 0x6B the address) in a standard TX segment, for 0xBB and 0xEB the address in a TX segment at the read width, the dummy cycles of the opcode, and an Rx segment of the drawn length at the read width. Each TX segment has its own TXDATA words. Read the RX words.
. Handshake run. After a cold reset write BASE = 0x10000000 (the SRAM base) and LIMIT = 0x10BFFFFF (the end of the SPI window, `memory_map.adoc`) and RANGE_VALID = 1. Write ADDR_SPACE_ID = 0x77 and both ADDR_HI registers = 0. Fill the destination footprint (n*C bytes) with sentinel words. Write SRC_ADDR_LO = 0x10B00024 with SRC_CONFIG INCREMENT=0 and WRAP=1, DST_ADDR_LO in SRAM with DST_CONFIG INCREMENT=1 and WRAP=0, TRANSFER_WIDTH = 2 (4 bytes), TOTAL_DATA_SIZE = n*C and CHUNK_DATA_SIZE = C. Write HANDSHAKE_INTR_ENABLE.MASK = 0x001 plus the drawn mask bits 10:1. Write CONTROL with OPCODE 0, HARDWARE_HANDSHAKE_ENABLE=1, INITIAL_TRANSFER=1 and GO=1. Run the SPI sequence with an Rx segment of n*C + 4*(W+1) bytes. The extra 4*(W+1) bytes keep the FIFO past W at every chunk.
. Handshake run. Write INTR_ENABLE.DMA_CHUNK_DONE=1 before GO. Poll in a loop with a bounded wait: read STATUS (log CHUNK_DONE) and read the last destination word, until the last word of chunk n differs from the sentinel. A timeout fails with `FAIL-DMA-TIMEOUT`. Measure the poll time from the GO write to that point as t_fill, in clocks. Read the destination (CHK-HS-DATA). Read CONTROL.GO and STATUS. Wait as long as t_fill, read CONTROL.GO again and keep polling STATUS for CHUNK_DONE over the same wait; bit 9 of `sep_internal_interrupts_probe_o` is sampled at every clock from before GO to the end of that wait (CHK-HS-GO, CHK-HS-NOCHUNK). Clear GO to stop the handshake operation. Log `dma_busy_probe_o` and `spi_tx_qd_probe_o`.
. Masked run. After a cold reset repeat step 5 with MASK bit 0 clear (the mask bits 10:1 stay as drawn). Do not wait for the SPI segment to end, because a masked DMA leaves the RX FIFO full. Sample `spi_lsio_trigger_probe_o` at every clock of a window as long as t_fill from the handshake run, and poll the destination. At the end of the window read the whole destination and CONTROL (CHK-HS-MASKED).
. Stop run. After a cold reset repeat step 5 with an Rx segment of n_stop*C + 4*(W+1) bytes, TOTAL_DATA_SIZE = T*C with T = n_stop + ceil(4*(W+1)/C) + 1 chunks, and a destination footprint of T*C bytes. The segment holds n_stop*C/4 + W + 1 words. These words fill at most n_stop + floor(4*(W+1)/C) chunks, also when the DMA drains every word of the segment, so at least one chunk of the total stays unmoved for every legal C, W and n_stop (for example, C = 4 and W = 7 fill at most n_stop + 8 chunks of T = n_stop + 9). Wait until STATUS.ACTIVE reads 0, then read the destination twice, separated by a wait as long as t_fill. When the two images are equal, count the chunks whose words all differ from the sentinel as n_moved. If n_moved is 0 or equals TOTAL_DATA_SIZE / C, fail with CTRL-MISSING.
. Stop run. Write CONTROL with GO=0 and the other fields kept. Run the SPI sequence again with an Rx segment of 4*(W+1) bytes. Sample `spi_lsio_trigger_probe_o` at every clock from the GO clear for a window as long as t_fill. Do not wait for the segment end, because the RX FIFO can fill. Read the destination at the end of the window (CHK-HS-STOP).
. Normal-mode control. After a cold reset write BASE = 0x10000000, LIMIT = 0x1003FFFF (the SEP SRAM, `memory_map.adoc`) and RANGE_VALID = 1. Write ADDR_SPACE_ID = 0x77 and both ADDR_HI registers = 0. Fill a 4096-byte source in the SRAM with unique words and a 4096-byte destination with sentinel words, both at addresses that are multiples of 4, disjoint and inside the range. Write SRC_ADDR_LO, DST_ADDR_LO, TOTAL_DATA_SIZE = 4096, CHUNK_DATA_SIZE = 1024 (the chunk shape of this control is a test parameter), TRANSFER_WIDTH = 2, and SRC_CONFIG and DST_CONFIG with INCREMENT=1 and WRAP=0. Write INTR_ENABLE with DMA_CHUNK_DONE=1 and the other bits 0. Write CONTROL with OPCODE 0, HARDWARE_HANDSHAKE_ENABLE=0, INITIAL_TRANSFER=1 and GO=1.
. Normal-mode control. Poll STATUS with a bounded wait until DONE=1 and BUSY=0. A timeout fails with `FAIL-DMA-TIMEOUT`. Log CHUNK_DONE at every poll. From the GO write to the end of the poll, sample bit 9 of `sep_internal_interrupts_probe_o` at every clock and count its rises. When STATUS reads CHUNK_DONE=1, DONE=0 and BUSY=0, write 1 to STATUS.CHUNK_DONE, write CONTROL with OPCODE 0, HARDWARE_HANDSHAKE_ENABLE=0, GO=1 and INITIAL_TRANSFER=0, and wait with a bound until `dma_busy_probe_o` has risen since that write before the next evaluation.
. Normal-mode control. After DONE read CONTROL.GO.

*Checkers*:

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|CHK-HS-DATA
|The first n*C/4 destination words equal the flash bytes at address 0 packed with `ByteOrder = 1` (the first byte received lands in RXDATA[7:0]; OpenTitan SPI_HOST theory of operation, "Input and Output Byte Ordering"), word for word and in order, with no duplicate and no missing word (`secure_dma.adoc`, CONTROL.HARDWARE_HANDSHAKE_ENABLE: read data from the source, copy it to the destination). A DUT that re-reads the FIFO word, skips a word or reorders chunks shows a mismatch, because the flash words are unique. The golden comes from the flash content and not from a DUT read; the reference run is a logged control. Anchored on frontdoor reads of the destination memory. Control: the n*C/4 reference words are pairwise distinct, else CTRL-MISSING, so the reference read through the SPI host keeps the uniqueness of the flash words. Evidence: `CHK-HS-DATA PASS seed=<n> width=x1 bytes=<b> words=<w> chunks=<n> chunk=<C> wm=<W> mask=0x<m> mismatch=0 first_mismatch_word=- ref_distinct=1 dest=<words>`.
|CHK-HS-MASKED
|With mask bit 0 clear, the destination keeps its sentinel while the trigger is high, and CONTROL.GO reads 1 at the end of the window (`secure_dma.adoc`, HANDSHAKE_INTR_ENABLE: enable bits for the handshake wires; CONTROL.GO: in handshake operation the engine does not clear GO, and firmware clears it to stop; no chunk moves in this window). A DUT that ignores the mask fills the destination; a DUT that ends the transfer on a masked trigger reads GO=0. Anchored on frontdoor reads of the destination and CONTROL, and on `spi_lsio_trigger_probe_o`. Control: the trigger probe is high for at least one clock in the window, and the mask=1 run in the same seed fills the destination. Evidence: `CHK-HS-MASKED PASS seed=<n> dest_sentinel_kept=1 go=1 ... control_mask1_filled=1 trigger_high_clk=<t>`.
|CHK-HS-GO
|In hardware handshake operation the engine does not clear GO; firmware clears it to stop (`secure_dma.adoc`, CONTROL.GO: "For Hardware handshake operation, DMA engine does not auto clear the Go bit. Firmware shall clear the Go bit when it intends to stop the hardware handshake operation."). GO reads 1 in the stop run after n_moved chunks below the total, immediately before the firmware clear. In the handshake run, after the destination holds the words of n chunks (= TOTAL_DATA_SIZE), GO reads 1, and still reads 1 after a wait as long as t_fill; then the leaf clears GO. A DUT that clears GO itself in handshake operation fails. STATUS.DONE at the total is logged (`OBS-HS-DONE seed=<n> done_at_total=<d> go_at_total=<g>`). Anchored on frontdoor reads of CONTROL and STATUS. Control: the normal-mode copy of the same seed reads GO=0 at completion. Evidence: `CHK-HS-GO PASS seed=<n> go_held_between_chunks=1 go_at_total=1 done_at_total=<d> go_after_wait=1 control_normal_go=0`.
|CHK-HS-STOP
|After firmware clears GO, the destination words after the moved chunks keep their sentinel while further Rx data holds the trigger high, and the words of the moved chunks equal the words read before the clear (`secure_dma.adoc`, CONTROL.GO: firmware clears GO to stop handshake operation). A DUT that keeps serving the trigger after the GO clear fills more words or writes over the moved chunks. Anchored on frontdoor reads of the destination and on `spi_lsio_trigger_probe_o`. Control: CONTROL.GO reads 1 immediately before the clear write, the chunks before the clear are filled (n_moved at least 1), at least one chunk of the total is unmoved (n_moved below TOTAL_DATA_SIZE / C, else CTRL-MISSING), and the trigger probe is high in the window. Evidence: `CHK-HS-STOP PASS seed=<n> n_stop=<k> n_moved=<m> chunks_total=<c> dest_after_clear_sentinel=1 trigger_high_clk=<t> ... go_before_clear=1 ... moved_unchanged=1`.
|CHK-HS-NOCHUNK
|STATUS.CHUNK_DONE reads 0 at every poll during the handshake run, and with INTR_ENABLE.DMA_CHUNK_DONE=1 bit 9 of `sep_internal_interrupts_probe_o` (PIC source 10) does not rise and is not high at any clock from before GO to the end of the t_fill wait (`secure_dma.adoc`, STATUS.CHUNK_DONE: raised only for multi-chunk memory-to-memory transfers; `dma.adoc`, Completion Reporting). A DUT that raises it per trigger shows 1 at a poll or, for a pulse shorter than the poll period, on the line. Anchored on STATUS readback and on the interrupt line. Control: the multi-chunk normal-mode copy in the same seed, polled the same way, reads CHUNK_DONE=1 at least once, or bit 9 of `sep_internal_interrupts_probe_o` (PIC source 10, with INTR_ENABLE.DMA_CHUNK_DONE=1) rises at least once during it, else CTRL-MISSING. The probe is part of the control because CHUNK_DONE clears when the next chunk starts, so a poll can miss a short pulse (`secure_dma.adoc`, STATUS.CHUNK_DONE, INTR_STATE.DMA_CHUNK_DONE). Evidence: `CHK-HS-NOCHUNK PASS seed=<n> polls_with_chunk_done=0 polls=<p> chunk_irq_rises=0 chunk_irq_high_clks=0 control_normal_polls=<m> control_chunk_irq_rises=<r>`.
|CHK-HS-TRIG-EQ
|With TX_WATERMARK 0 the trigger `lsio_trigger_o` is `RXQD >= RX_WATERMARK` (`hw/sys/sep/doc/spi.adoc`, DMA Trigger). With RXQD = W_eq-1 the trigger probe reads low and did not rise; the next word makes RXQD = W_eq, and the probe rises exactly once, after the last sample edge of that word, and reads high. A host whose trigger needs RXQD above the watermark stays low at equality and fails; a host that rises one word early fails the first read. Anchor: `spi_lsio_trigger_probe_o` at every clock, the pad sampler and STATUS readback (approved). Controls: STATUS.RXQD reads W_eq-1 and then W_eq, else CTRL-MISSING. Evidence: `CHK-HS-TRIG-EQ PASS seed=<n> width=x1 bytes=<4*W_eq> wm=<W_eq> chunk=<4*W_eq> rxqd_below=<W_eq-1> trig_below=0 rises_below=0 rxqd_at=<W_eq> trig_at=1 rises_at=1 ... after_last_bit=1 expect_below=0 expect_at=1`.
|CHK-HS-LANE-RX
|For each lane read, the RXDATA bytes the host delivered, unpacked with `ByteOrder = 1`, equal the seeded flash bytes at its address (Rules of this section, Lane data). A host that samples the wrong lane or packs the bytes in another order fails. Anchor: RXDATA readback (approved). Evidence: `CHK-HS-LANE-RX PASS seed=<n> width=x<w> opcode=0x<op> addr=0x<a> bytes=<b> expected=<hex> delivered=<hex>` per read.
|CHK-SPI-SB-RX
|Per session (`hw/sys/sep/dv/cocotb/env/sep_spi_scoreboard.py`, `expect_rx`): for every frame of the session, the bytes the DUT delivered equal the flash record's `data_out` and the monitor's wire decode at the lane width of the command. Evidence: `CHK-SPI-SB-RX PASS seed=<n> frames=<n> bytes=<n>` per session.
|CHK-SPI-SESSION-FRAMES
|Per session: the monitor saw at least one frame, and every frame carries one scoreboard expectation whose bytes cover its RX segment (the handshake frame: the moved chunks). A frame with no expectation, or a short expectation, fails. Evidence: `CHK-SPI-SESSION-FRAMES PASS seed=<n> session=<tag> frames=<n> expectations=<n> widths=<list> bytes=<n> short=-`.
|CHK-SPI-WIRE
|Per session, the wire and output-enable checks of `OcahSpiMonitor`, the flash ledger and the frame shape (CHK-SPI-FRAME-SHAPE, CHK-SPI-LEDGER), as in <<sep_spi_ot_dma_rx_test,entry 4.2>>.
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every named checker PASSes. UVM_ERROR == 0.

*Scope limit*: The open tests `sep_spi_ot_dma_rx_test` and `sep_spi_ot_dma_tx_test` keep the single handshake configuration with mask bit 0 set. The TX-direction trigger wait is not graded anywhere: `sep_spi_ot_dma_tx_test` logs it (OBS-MULTICHUNK), because no source states one trigger per chunk in the TX direction. Not claimed: the interrupt clear write (the leaf does not drive it), handshake inputs 1 to 10 (tied low), BUSY before the GO clear, the count of beats per chunk, the words beyond n*C/4 in the handshake run, the number of chunks that the DMA moves before the stop (the leaf requires at least one unmoved chunk), and STATUS.RXWM at RXQD equal to RX_WATERMARK. In the handshake, masked and stop runs C/4 stays at or below W, so both readings of the watermark leave at least C/4 words when the trigger fires; the trigger cell grades the trigger at equality, where `spi.adoc` (DMA Trigger) states `>=`.

'''''

[[sep_dma_zero_fill_rand_test]]
===== 4.16 `sep_dma_zero_fill_rand_test`

*Phase*: 3

*Area*: SPI and DMA

*Run mode*: `no_cpu` (`lsu_stub_all_live`), `+skip_fuse_sense`. 4 seeds. Verilator and VCS; testlist groups `cpu_stub` and `all`, file `system.toml`, one testlist entry.

*Source Module*: `sep_dma_zero_fill_rand_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip register frontdoor over `s_axi` (also for every SRAM image). Approved probes: `dma_busy_probe_o`, `sep_internal_interrupts_probe_o` (bits 8 and 10, PIC sources 9 and 11) and `spi_lsio_trigger_probe_o`. Observation taps: `dma_axi_req_probe_o` and `dma_status_probe_o`. Model: `env/sep_dma_model.DmaZeroFill`. No inject.

*Randomization*: `SepSeededRng` from the run seed. Leg Z: the order of the nine {destination mode} x {TRANSFER_WIDTH encoding} cells, per cell the chunk size (a multiple of the width), the chunk count (one for the fixed destination, two or more for WRAP), the size of the last chunk (so the last write can be narrower than the width), the start offset inside the 64-bit beat, the placement and the sentinel words. Leg W: the range size (64 to 256 bytes), the guards, the distances below BASE and past LIMIT, the crossing chunk size and the trim of an unaligned LIMIT. Leg A: the fill size (4 KB to 8 KB) and the abort point as a fraction (20 to 80 percent) of the clean time of the same fill. Leg L: the range and the overrun. Every value is drawn before the first stimulus.

*Objective*: The zero fill holds its contract (`dma.hjson`, CONTROL.OPCODE; `hw/sys/sep/doc/dma.adoc`, Zero Fill): it writes zeros to the destination and never reads the source, in every destination mode and width; it writes only inside the enabled memory range, checked before every write; it is rejected with OPCODE_ERROR in hardware handshake mode; and it aborts and recovers like a copy.

*Test Coverage*: zero fills in INCREMENT, INCREMENT and WRAP, and fixed destination modes at widths 1, 2 and 4, single and multi-chunk, with narrow last writes and unaligned starts; a fill straight after a copy; fills that start below BASE, end past LIMIT in one chunk, cross LIMIT in a later chunk and end on an unaligned LIMIT; a copy outside the range; OPCODE 5; a zero fill with hardware handshake; ABORT during a fill; a locked range. Functional coverage: `sep_dma_zero_cg`.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Draw every value from the seed and log it in `DRAW` lines. Bring up with the CPU held off. Write BASE and LIMIT around the SEP SRAM, RANGE_VALID = 1, ADDR_SPACE_ID = 0x77 and both ADDR_HI registers = 0.
. Leg Z, per cell: fill the destination span and its guards with drawn words that have no zero byte. Copy eight non-zero words between two other SRAM blocks, the destination block zeroed first, and read the destination block back. Program the fill (SRC_ADDR_LO = 0, the destination, the sizes, the width and DST_CONFIG) and write CONTROL with OPCODE 4, INITIAL_TRANSFER=1 and GO=1. Wait for DONE with the re-GO rule of `sep_dma_abort_recovery_rand_test`, step 3. Read the destination span, STATUS and ERROR_CODE (CHK-DMA-ZERO-IMAGE, CHK-DMA-ZERO-BEAT, CHK-DMA-ZERO-RESIDUE).
. Leg W, with INTR_ENABLE = DMA_DONE | DMA_ERROR: fill the whole range (CHK-DMA-ZERO-WIN-OK, CHK-DMA-ZERO-IRQ); fill from below BASE at width 4 and, with BASE one byte above the start, at width 1; fill past LIMIT in one chunk (CHK-DMA-ZERO-IRQ) and across LIMIT in a later chunk (CHK-DMA-ZERO-WIN-ERR each); fill to an unaligned LIMIT (CHK-DMA-ZERO-WIN-EXACT); copy to a destination outside the range (CHK-DMA-ZERO-COPY-CTRL). Write 1 to STATUS.ERROR after each error and wait for BUSY=0.
. Leg E: write OPCODE 5 with GO, then after the clear a zero fill (CHK-DMA-ZERO-OPC). Write SPI CONTROL.TX_WATERMARK = 1 with the TX FIFO empty and HANDSHAKE_INTR_ENABLE = 1, wait for the trigger probe, and write a zero fill with HARDWARE_HANDSHAKE_ENABLE=1 (CHK-DMA-ZERO-HS). Restore both registers.
. Leg A: measure the clean time of a fill of the drawn size, refill the sentinel, start the fill again and write CONTROL with ABORT=1 after the drawn fraction of that time. A write that finds BUSY=0 or that ends with DONE is repeated with the next drawn fraction, at most three times, then CTRL-MISSING. Read the destination, wait a quarter of the clean time and read it again, write 1 to STATUS.ABORTED, then run a recovery fill (CHK-DMA-ZERO-ABORT).
. Leg L: write the range, write MuBi4 False to RANGE_REGWEN, write a wider LIMIT, read LIMIT back and run a fill past the locked LIMIT (CHK-DMA-ZERO-WIN-LOCK).

*Checkers*:

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|CHK-DMA-ZERO-IMAGE
|Every word of the destination span equals the model: the bytes of the model writes are 0 and every other byte keeps its drawn value; DONE=1, ERROR=0 and ERROR_CODE=0. A DUT that writes the wrong bytes, too few or too many fails the compare. Evidence: `CHK-DMA-ZERO-IMAGE PASS seed=<n> mode=<m> enc=<e> ... mismatches=0`.
|CHK-DMA-ZERO-BEAT
|No AR handshake in the window of the fill; one AW per model write with AxLEN 0, AxADDR[31:2] and WSTRB equal to the model; one OKAY B per AW. A DUT that reads the source shows AR beats. Control: the 4-byte-wide copy of eight words before the fill records eight AR handshakes at the eight source word addresses through the same tap, else CTRL-MISSING. This proves the AR decode of the tap live for every `ar=0` result of the leaf. Each fill or copy window also fails (`DMA-TAP-WINDOW FAIL`) unless the tap sampled every clock from GO to BUSY=0, so an empty AR or AW list comes from a sampled window. Evidence: `CHK-DMA-ZERO-BEAT PASS seed=<n> ... ar=0 copy_ar=8 aw=<k> beats=<k> shape=model`.
|CHK-DMA-ZERO-RESIDUE
|The bytes the fill writes read 0 although the last word of the copy before it is non-zero. A DUT that writes its data register unchanged writes that word. Control: the copy destination, zeroed before the copy, reads back equal to the eight non-zero source words, else CTRL-MISSING, so the copy data passed through the DMA datapath before the fill. Evidence: `CHK-DMA-ZERO-RESIDUE PASS seed=<n> ... copy_last=<w> copy_dst_last=<w> cleared_bytes=<b>`.
|CHK-DMA-ZERO-WIN-OK
|A fill of the whole range completes and clears it; the guards outside the range keep their words.
|CHK-DMA-ZERO-WIN-ERR
|Per out-of-range fill: ERROR=1 and ERROR_CODE equals DST_ADDR_ERROR alone; no AR handshake; one AW per model write inside the range (none when the first write is outside) with AxLEN 0, AxADDR[31:2] and WSTRB equal to the model, and no other AW or W handshake; one OKAY B per AW; those bytes read 0 and every other byte keeps its word. A DUT that checks only the first chunk writes past LIMIT in the crossing case; a DUT that writes outside the range in place of an in-range write fails the per-write address compare.
|CHK-DMA-ZERO-WIN-EXACT
|A fill whose narrow last write ends on an unaligned LIMIT completes with ERROR=0, and the bytes after LIMIT keep their words. A DUT that checks whole words rejects this fill.
|CHK-DMA-ZERO-COPY-CTRL
|Control: a copy whose destination is outside the range completes with the data copied, so the range bounds a fill and not a copy.
|CHK-DMA-ZERO-IRQ
|PIC sources 9 and 11 are low before each graded fill, so a line left high fails. PIC source 9 rises and PIC source 11 does not for the fill of the whole range; PIC source 11 rises and PIC source 9 does not for the fill past LIMIT.
|CHK-DMA-ZERO-OPC
|OPCODE 5 sets ERROR with ERROR_CODE equal to OPCODE_ERROR alone, moves no beat and leaves the destination; after the write of 1 to STATUS.ERROR a zero fill completes.
|CHK-DMA-ZERO-HS
|A zero fill with HARDWARE_HANDSHAKE_ENABLE=1 sets ERROR with ERROR_CODE equal to OPCODE_ERROR alone, moves no beat and leaves the destination. Control: the trigger probe reads 1 before the GO write (CTRL-MISSING otherwise).
|CHK-DMA-ZERO-ABORT
|ABORTED reads 1; the destination is whole zero words followed by sentinel words, at least one of each, and reads the same a quarter of the clean time later; ABORTED reads 0 after its write of 1; the recovery fill completes and clears the whole destination.
|CHK-DMA-ZERO-WIN-LOCK
|After MuBi4 False is written to RANGE_REGWEN, RANGE_REGWEN reads other than True and a later write to LIMIT leaves it unchanged; a fill past LIMIT then ends with DST_ADDR_ERROR, with the AR, AW, W and B shape of CHK-DMA-ZERO-WIN-ERR for the writes up to LIMIT and no write beyond it.
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every named checker PASSes. UVM_ERROR == 0.

*Scope limit*: Not claimed: the AR and AW handshakes after the ABORTED rise (logged as `OBS-DMA-ZERO-ABORT`; `sep_dma_abort_recovery_rand_test` owns that contract), ICCM and DCCM destinations (`sep_dma_zero_test`, because the CPU stub of this target answers no request on the core's DMA port), and the SoC address spaces, which SEP ties off.

'''''

[[sep_dma_zero_test]]
===== 4.17 `sep_dma_zero_test`

*Phase*: 3

*Area*: SPI and DMA

*Run mode*: `cpu`, `+skip_fuse_sense`

*Source Module*: `sep_dma_zero_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip, CPU FW (`fw/tests/dma_zero_test`), `tb/sep_outbound_mbx.sv` (console and verdict mailbox)

*Objective*: The zero fill clears ICCM and DCCM through the core's DMA slave port, which the no_cpu target does not model, and the core's refusal of a write narrower than a word reaches software as BUS_ERROR.

*Test Coverage*: zero fills into SEP SRAM at widths 4 and 1, into a DCCM buffer and into ICCM; a 2-byte-wide fill into ICCM. Functional coverage: `sep_dma_zero_cg.cp_tcm_outcome`, sampled while the leaf holds its graded window from the CPU release to the end of the firmware run.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Write BASE = 0, LIMIT = 0xFFFFFFFF and RANGE_VALID = 1.
. SRAM: fill 18 words with a pattern, zero the inner 16 at width 4, and check them with the CPU; fill four words, zero bytes 1 to 7 at width 1 and check every byte.
. DCCM: fill an 18-word buffer of the firmware with the pattern, zero its inner 16 words at width 4, and check them with the CPU.
. ICCM: copy the pattern to 18 ICCM words, zero the inner 16 at width 4, copy the 18 words back to a sentinel-filled SRAM block and check them with the CPU.
. Narrow: check DMA_BUS_ERR_STATUS reads 0, zero 8 ICCM bytes at width 2, read STATUS, ERROR_CODE and DMA_BUS_ERR_STATUS, clear both, and zero the same bytes at width 4.

*Checkers*:

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|CHK-ZERO-SRAM
|Each fill ends with DONE and ERROR_CODE 0; the words and bytes it covers read 0 and their neighbours keep the pattern.
|CHK-ZERO-DCCM
|The fill ends with DONE and ERROR_CODE 0; the inner 16 words of the DCCM buffer read 0 and the first and last keep the pattern.
|CHK-ZERO-ICCM
|The three transfers end with DONE and ERROR_CODE 0; the copy back shows the inner 16 words 0 and the first and last with the pattern.
|CHK-ZERO-TCM-NARROW
|The 2-byte-wide fill ends with ERROR, ERROR_CODE equal to BUS_ERROR alone and DMA_BUS_ERR_STATUS equal to the host-path bit alone. After the DMA_BUS_ERR_CLEAR write and the STATUS RW1C write, and before the next GO, DMA_BUS_ERR_STATUS reads 0, STATUS.ERROR reads 0 and ERROR_CODE reads 0 (`secure_dma.adoc`, STATUS.ERROR: RW1C; `dma.hjson`, ERROR_CODE: cleared by the RW1C write of STATUS.ERROR). A DUT whose RW1C clear does not land shows ERROR or ERROR_CODE set at that read, which a check after the next GO cannot see. The 4-byte-wide fill then completes.
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every named checker PASSes, and the firmware returns 0. UVM_ERROR == 0.

'''''

[[sep_dma_zero_fill_continuation_test]]
===== 4.18 `sep_dma_zero_fill_continuation_test`

*Phase*: 3

*Area*: SPI and DMA

*Run mode*: `no_cpu` (`lsu_stub_all_live`), `+skip_fuse_sense`. 1 seed; the stimulus does not depend on the seed. Verilator and VCS; testlist groups `cpu_stub` and `all`, file `system.toml`, one testlist entry.

*Source Module*: `sep_dma_zero_fill_continuation_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip register frontdoor over `s_axi` (also for every SRAM image). Approved probe: `spi_lsio_trigger_probe_o`. Observation taps: `dma_axi_req_probe_o` and `dma_status_probe_o` (AW address and WSTRB of each write). No inject.

*Randomization*: None. Directed.

*Objective*: The range and handshake rules of the zero fill hold when a fill is continued by a CONTROL write of GO=1 with INITIAL_TRANSFER=0 (`hw/sys/sep/doc/dma.adoc`, Zero Fill). The fill writes only inside BASE to LIMIT inclusive, each write is checked before it is issued, and the first write that would leave the range is not issued and ends in DST_ADDR_ERROR; locking the range with RANGE_REGWEN fixes the erase window until reset. With HARDWARE_HANDSHAKE_ENABLE set a zero fill ends in OPCODE_ERROR when the first trigger arrives. Neither rule has a condition on INITIAL_TRANSFER.

*Test Coverage*: the continuation after a fill whose last write ends at LIMIT, at TRANSFER_WIDTH encodings 2, 1 and 0; the continuation with HARDWARE_HANDSHAKE_ENABLE set after chunk 1 ran without it; the continuation after the range is narrowed and locked between chunks. Functional coverage: `sep_dma_zero_cg.cp_cont_outcome`, sampled only while the leaf holds its graded window over each of the five graded continuations; the control fills run with the window closed.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Write ADDR_SPACE_ID = 0x77, both ADDR_HI registers = 0 and INTR_ENABLE = 0. Write sentinel words with no zero byte around each window (16 bytes on each side).
. Leg E, per width encoding 2, 1, 0: set the range to a 64-byte window and run a two-chunk zero fill of the whole window with INITIAL_TRANSFER=1 to DONE (control). Write GO=1 with INITIAL_TRANSFER=0 and no other register write. Wait until BUSY=0 or ERROR, read ERROR_CODE and the window with its guards (CHK-DMA-ZERO-CONT-LIMIT).
. Leg H: set the range to the SEP SRAM and program a 64-byte fill in two 32-byte chunks at width 4. Run chunk 1 with HARDWARE_HANDSHAKE_ENABLE=0 to CHUNK_DONE (control). Hold the SPI trigger high (SPI CONTROL.TX_WATERMARK 1 with an empty TX FIFO, `spi.adoc`, DMA Trigger) and unmask trigger 0 in HANDSHAKE_INTR_ENABLE. Write CONTROL with OPCODE 4, HARDWARE_HANDSHAKE_ENABLE=1, INITIAL_TRANSFER=0 and GO=1. Wait for DONE or ERROR and read ERROR_CODE (CHK-DMA-ZERO-CONT-HS). Clear the DMA state, then write the same CONTROL value with INITIAL_TRANSFER=1 (control).
. Leg L, last because the lock holds until reset: set a 128-byte range and program a 128-byte fill in two 64-byte chunks at width 4. Run chunk 1 to CHUNK_DONE (control). Narrow LIMIT to the end of chunk 1, write MuBi4 False to RANGE_REGWEN, write the wide LIMIT again and read LIMIT and RANGE_REGWEN (control). Write GO=1 with INITIAL_TRANSFER=0. Wait for DONE or ERROR, read ERROR_CODE and the window with its guards (CHK-DMA-ZERO-CONT-LOCK). Clear the DMA state and run a new 128-byte fill with INITIAL_TRANSFER=1 (control).

*Checkers*:

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|CHK-DMA-ZERO-CONT-LIMIT
|Per width: after the continuation no write byte (AxADDR and WSTRB lanes on `dma_axi_req_probe_o`) is outside BASE to LIMIT, and the sentinel words outside the window read unchanged. A DUT that does not check a write of the continuation against the range writes past LIMIT and shows the write on the probe and in the guard readback. Control: the fill before it writes every byte of the window once, ends with DONE and ERROR_CODE 0 and leaves the guards, so the probe and the readback see writes up to LIMIT. Evidence: `CHK-DMA-ZERO-CONT-LIMIT PASS seed=<n> w=<w> ... bytes_outside=0 first_outside=none guard_words_changed=[]`.
|CHK-DMA-ZERO-CONT-HS
|The continuation with HARDWARE_HANDSHAKE_ENABLE=1 sets ERROR with ERROR_CODE equal to OPCODE_ERROR alone and issues no write. A DUT that uses the handshake setting of the first GO runs chunk 2 and shows its writes. Controls: chunk 1 completes with the handshake off; the trigger probe reads 1 at the GO write; the same CONTROL value with INITIAL_TRANSFER=1 gives OPCODE_ERROR and no write, so the error is one the engine reports. Evidence: `CHK-DMA-ZERO-CONT-HS PASS seed=<n> ... error_code=0x<OPCODE_ERROR> ... writes=[]`.
|CHK-DMA-ZERO-CONT-LOCK
|The continuation after the range is narrowed to chunk 1 and locked issues no write byte outside the locked window, zeroes no word outside it, and stops with ERROR and ERROR_CODE equal to DST_ADDR_ERROR alone, because its first write is outside the window. A DUT that uses the range of the first GO zeroes chunk 2. Controls: chunk 1 completes inside the wide range; RANGE_REGWEN reads other than True and LIMIT keeps the narrow value through a later write; a new fill with INITIAL_TRANSFER=1 stops at the locked LIMIT with DST_ADDR_ERROR. Evidence: `CHK-DMA-ZERO-CONT-LOCK PASS seed=<n> ... error_code=0x<DST_ADDR_ERROR> ... bytes_outside=0 ... words_zeroed_outside=0`.
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every named checker PASSes on every leg and every control holds (a control that does not hold fails with CTRL-MISSING). UVM_ERROR == 0.

*Scope limit*: Not claimed: the outcome of the continuation in Leg E other than the range bound. `hw/sys/sep/doc/dma.adoc` (Zero Fill) states that a start with no bytes left ends in `ERROR_CODE.SIZE_ERROR` without a write; the leaf logs `ERROR_CODE` for that continuation and does not grade it. Copies continued by GO=1 with INITIAL_TRANSFER=0 are not claimed.

'''''

[[sep_spi_pad_oe_rules_test]]
===== 4.19 `sep_spi_pad_oe_rules_test`

*Phase*: 3

*Area*: SPI and DMA

*Run mode*: `no_cpu` (`lsu_stub_all_live`) with real fuse sense of the committed default image (no `+skip_fuse_sense`), because the leaf asserts `rst_ni`. 3 seeds; tool: both (Verilator and VCS); the reset-window check is graded on VCS only; testlist groups `cpu_stub` and `all`, file `spi.toml`.

*Source Module*: `sep_spi_pad_oe_rules_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_axi_vip` for the register frontdoor over `s_axi` (CPU-LSU splice, approved). A per-clock sampler reads `rst_ni`, `dbg_sep_reset_n_o`, `spi_cs_n_o`, `spi_sck_o` and the output-enable taps `spi_cs_oe_o`, `spi_sck_oe_o` and `spi_sd_oe_o[3:0]` of `tb_top` (approved, `SEP_TB_ARCH.adoc`, SPI host pads) on every rising edge of `clk_i`, in the read-only phase. It drives nothing. The leaf names the window that each sample belongs to; a sample outside every window is not graded. No responder: the RX segments receive the testbench default. No proposed probe. No inject. No check is blocked.

*Randomization*: `SepSeededRng` from the run seed (RAND-REP). Dimensions: CONFIGOPTS (CLKDIV 1 to 3, CSNLEAD, CSNTRAIL and CSNIDLE 0 to 3); the order of the six frames of each traffic leg; per frame the TX bytes (1 to 8) or the RX length (1 to 8), and in the chain the TX byte and the dummy length (2 to 6 cycles); the length of the segment that the reset cuts (16 to 32 bytes), the `rst_ni` point (in sck rises) and the wait after the release; the length of the OUTPUT_EN = 0 segment (8 to 32 bytes). `reseed = 3` samples other orders, lengths and reset points. The legs and their windows are the same on every seed.

*Objective*: The output-enable rules of the SEP SPI host hold (`smu-sep.adoc`, "SEP SPI Host Pin Assignment"; `spi_controller.adoc`, CONTROL.OUTPUT_EN with reset 0; OpenTitan SPI_HOST theory of operation, "SPI_HOST Output Enable"). The host drives no pad until software sets CONTROL.OUTPUT_EN, so all output enables are 0 from a cold reset to the first OUTPUT_EN write, and with OUTPUT_EN = 0 also while SPIEN = 1 and the host runs a command. The host drives a data lane only while chip select is asserted. With CONTROL.OUTPUT_EN = 1, `cs_oe` and `sck_oe` are 1 with chip select high or low (`spi_host.hjson:222-224`, CONTROL.OUTPUT_EN: "Enable the SPI host output buffers for the sck, csb, and sd lines."); the pin-assignment text narrows only the data lanes.

*Test Coverage*: All output enables in the cold-reset window that cuts an x1 TX segment (graded on VCS); all output enables with OUTPUT_EN = 0 while a TX command runs; the data lane output enables in every chip-select-high sample of two traffic legs, including the clocks right after each chip-select rise and the gaps between commands; `cs_oe` and `sck_oe` in every chip-select-low and every chip-select-high sample of the traffic legs. The traffic legs hold TX x1, TX x2, TX x4, RX x1, RX x4 and a CSAAT chain (TX x1, dummy, RX x4). The per-segment output-enable pattern is graded in <<sep_spi_pad_speed_dir_matrix_test,entry 4.7>>; the monitor checks `oe_idle` and `oe_ctrl` grade the same rules at frame edges in the tests that attach `OcahSpiMonitor`. Functional coverage: `sep_spi_lane_oe_win_cg` (`cp_oe_window`: `cold_reset` on VCS, `output_en_off`, `cs_high`, `cs_low`).

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Draw the plan from the seed and log it (`PLAN ...`, `DRAW seed=<n> ...`). Load the committed default fuse image and bring the DUT up. Start the sampler. Every wait in the steps below is a bounded wait; a timeout fails the check that the step feeds.
. Traffic A. Write CONFIGOPTS and CONTROL with SPIEN=1 and OUTPUT_EN=1. Run the six frames in the drawn order: write the TXDATA words of each TX segment (each TX segment its own words), write the COMMANDs (CSAAT on every segment of the chain but the last), wait until STATUS shows ACTIVE=0 and CMDQD=0, and read the RX words. The window is "traffic".
. Cold reset. Write the TXDATA words of a long x1 TX segment and its COMMAND. After the drawn number of sck rises with chip select low, take the last sample before `rst_ni` falls (control) and pulse `rst_ni` (`sep_base_test.pulse_rst_ni`, real fuse sense). The window "reset" opens at the first sample with `dbg_sep_reset_n_o` = 0 and closes before the first CONTROL write after the release and fuse sense.
. OUTPUT_EN = 0. Write CONTROL with SPIEN=1 and OUTPUT_EN=0, write the TXDATA words of a TX segment and its COMMAND. Read STATUS until a read shows ACTIVE=1 (at most 16 reads); wait until ACTIVE=0 and CMDQD=0. The window "off" covers the command and 64 clocks after it.
. Traffic B. Write CONTROL with SPIEN=1 and OUTPUT_EN=1 and run the six frames again in their own drawn order.
. Grade the windows.

*Checkers*:

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|CHK-SPI-OE-OFF
|Every sample of the OUTPUT_EN = 0 window reads `spi_cs_oe_o`, `spi_sck_oe_o` and `spi_sd_oe_o` all 0 (`smu-sep.adoc`, "SEP SPI Host Pin Assignment": the host drives no pad until software sets CONTROL.OUTPUT_EN). A host that drives a pad when SPIEN alone is set fails. On VCS an X or Z on an output enable also fails. Anchor: the output-enable taps (approved). Control: a frontdoor STATUS read inside the window shows ACTIVE=1, so the host runs the command and only the OUTPUT_EN gate keeps the pads undriven, and the window has samples, else CTRL-MISSING. Evidence: `CHK-SPI-OE-OFF PASS seed=<n> window_samples=<k> violations=0 xz=<k> ... status_active_in_window=1 tx_bytes=<b>`.
|CHK-SPI-OE-RESET
|VCS only: every sample of the cold-reset window reads all output enables 0, and an X or Z on an output enable fails (`smu-sep.adoc`, "SEP SPI Host Pin Assignment"; `spi_controller.adoc`, CONTROL.OUTPUT_EN resets to 0). A host that keeps a pad driven through the reset fails. On Verilator, which is two-state, the leaf logs `OBS-SPI-OE-RESET LOG seed=<n> ...` with the same counts and grades nothing, and the check does not count in k. Anchor: the output-enable taps and `dbg_sep_reset_n_o` (approved). Controls (both simulators): the last sample before `rst_ni` falls reads chip select low with `spi_cs_oe_o`, `spi_sck_oe_o` and `spi_sd_oe_o[0]` = 1, the SEP reset asserts in the hold, and the window has samples, else CTRL-MISSING (`CTL-SPI-OE-RESET LOG seed=<n> pre_reset_cs_n=0 cs_oe=1 sck_oe=1 sd_oe=<v> ...`). Evidence: `CHK-SPI-OE-RESET PASS seed=<n> window_samples=<k> violations=0 xz=0 ... sim=vcs`.
|CHK-SPI-OE-CS-HIGH
|Every traffic sample with chip select high, also the clocks right after each chip-select rise and the gaps between commands, reads `spi_sd_oe_o` = 0 (`smu-sep.adoc`, "SEP SPI Host Pin Assignment": the host drives a data lane only while chip select is asserted). Every such sample also reads `spi_cs_oe_o` = 1 and `spi_sck_oe_o` = 1 (`spi_host.hjson:222-224`). A host that holds a lane driven past the chip-select rise, or that turns off the chip-select or clock output enable while chip select is high, fails. Control: the count of traffic samples with chip select low and `spi_sd_oe_o[0]` = 1 is above zero, samples after a chip-select rise exist, and the OUTPUT_EN = 0 window (CHK-SPI-OE-OFF) has samples that read `spi_cs_oe_o` and `spi_sck_oe_o` 0 (`smu-sep.adoc:275-276`: the host "drives no pad until software sets its `CONTROL.OUTPUT_EN`"), else CTRL-MISSING. Evidence: `CHK-SPI-OE-CS-HIGH PASS seed=<n> cs_high_samples=<k> cs_high_after_rise=<k> cs_rises=<k> sd_oe_violations=0 cs_sck_samples=<k> expect_cs_sck_oe=(1, 1) cs_sck_oe_violations=0 control_sd0_high_cs_low=<k> control_off_samples=<k> control_off_violations=0 xz=<k> first=-`.
|CHK-SPI-OE-CS-LOW
|With CONTROL.OUTPUT_EN = 1, every traffic sample with chip select low reads `spi_cs_oe_o` = 1 and `spi_sck_oe_o` = 1 (`spi_host.hjson:222-224`, CONTROL.OUTPUT_EN). A host that lets go of chip select or the clock inside a frame fails. Control: the chip-select-low sample count is above zero, else CTRL-MISSING. Evidence: `CHK-SPI-OE-CS-LOW PASS seed=<n> cs_low_samples=<k> cs_sck_oe_violations=0 xz=<k> first=-`.
|===

An X or Z on a graded sample is a violation on VCS. Each check logs its sample count and its violation count.

*Pass Criteria*: Every named checker PASSes (CHK-SPI-OE-RESET on VCS only). UVM_ERROR == 0.

*Scope limit*: The output enables after a CONTROL.SW_RST abort are graded in <<sep_spi_fifo_stall_watermark_rand_test,entry 4.9>> (data lanes only), and the reset window in the middle of an x2 or x4 segment in <<sep_spi_cold_reset_values_rand_test,entry 4.11>>. The pad input enable, `lsio_disable`, `hw2_ovrd`, SPI.DATA[7:4], SPI.DQS and the DQS loopback are outside SEP (`smu-sep.adoc`, "SEP SPI Host Pin Assignment") and are not graded here. Samples between the `rst_ni` fall and the SEP reset are logged as lag samples and not graded.

'''''

[[sep-spi-lane-coverage]]
===== 4.20 SPI lane data and output-enable coverage statement

*Phase*: 3

*Area*: SPI and DMA

*Scope*: SEP DV grades the SPI function at the SEP boundary, `sep_io_spi_req_o` and `sep_io_spi_rsp_i` (`hw/sys/sep/rtl/sep.sv`), through the testbench taps of `SEP_TB_ARCH.adoc` (SPI host pads). SEP has no pad cells: `sep_io_pkg::ot_spi_pad_map` (`hw/sys/sep/rtl/sep_io_pkg.sv`) feeds the SMU and SMC pads. Pad delay, SCK frequency limits (`spi.adoc`, Features: design targets, not operating limits) and I/O timing margin are platform integration and are not graded in simulation. The host has no octal (x8), DDR or DQS function (`spi.adoc`, Features: "Standard (x1), dual (x2) and quad (x4)", "No octal (x8), DDR or DQS read-data strobe"), and SPI.DATA[4-7], SPI.DQS and the DQS loopback are not driven (`smu-sep.adoc`, "SEP SPI Host Pin Assignment"); SEP has no port for them, so they are outside this statement and their pads are an SMU and SMC concern.

*What is graded at x1, x2 and x4*. Each cell names the entries and checkers that grade it, byte for byte against seeded stimulus and the lane contract of the Rules of this section (Lane data).

[cols="2,3,3",options="header"]
|===
|Item |x1 |x2 and x4
|TX data on the lanes
|Entries 4.7, 4.8, 4.9 (CHK-SPI-SB-FRAME, CHK-SPI-LANE-DATA), 4.11 (CHK-SPI-SB-FRAME), 4.4 and 4.5 (CHK-SPI-SB-TX, program 0x02)
|x2: entries 4.7, 4.8, 4.9 and 4.11 (CHK-SPI-SB-FRAME). x4: the same, plus 4.4 and 4.5 (CHK-SPI-SB-TX, program 0x32). The flash model has no dual program command, so no flash test grades x2 TX
|RX data on the lanes and in RXDATA
|Entries 4.7, 4.8, 4.9 (CHK-SPI-SB-FRAME), 4.15 (CHK-SPI-SB-RX, CHK-HS-DATA, CHK-HS-LANE-RX), 4.2 (CHK-SPI-SB-RX, CHK-DATAPATH), 4.5 (CHK-SPI-SB-RX for the primary READ 0x03; its other x1 reads in firmware, CHK-READ and CHK-FAST-READ)
|Entries 4.7, 4.8, 4.9 (CHK-SPI-SB-FRAME), 4.15 (CHK-SPI-SB-RX, CHK-HS-LANE-RX), 4.2 (CHK-SPI-SB-RX, CHK-DATAPATH), 4.5 (CHK-SPI-SB-RX, CHK-SPI-LANE-READ for 0x3B, 0x6B, 0xBB, 0xEB)
|Data lane output enable per segment
|Entry 4.7 (CHK-SPI-OE-SEG): x1 TX and x1 bidirectional 0b0001. x1 RX-only and dummy segments are logged only (open item below)
|Entry 4.7 (CHK-SPI-OE-SEG): x2 TX 0b0011, x4 TX 0b1111, x2 and x4 RX 0b0000
|Output-enable rules
|Entry 4.19 (reset window on VCS, OUTPUT_EN = 0, chip select high, chip select low); `OcahSpiMonitor` `oe_idle`, `oe_lanes`, `oe_ctrl` in every test that attaches it
|The same, with traffic at x2 and x4; the reset window cut in an x2 or x4 segment in entry 4.11 (CHK-SPI-OE-RESET)
|Abort
|SW_RST: entry 4.9 (CHK-SPI-SWRST-ABORT). `rst_ni`: entry 4.11
|The same, at each width
|Chains that change width
|Entry 4.7 (CHK-SPI-CHAIN, CHK-SPI-SB-FRAME): the data of each segment at its own width, in one chip-select window
|Same
|Status, watermarks and events
|Entry 4.9 (CHK-SPI-TXSTALL, CHK-SPI-RXSTALL, CHK-SPI-WM, CHK-SPI-QD, CHK-SPI-CMDQD, CHK-SPI-DEPTH-TX, CHK-SPI-DEPTH-RX, CHK-SPI-DEPTH-CMD, CHK-SPI-EVENT-WM)
|Same, at each width
|DMA trigger at RXQD equal to RX_WATERMARK
|Entry 4.15 (CHK-HS-TRIG-EQ)
|Not width-dependent
|Rx sampling point with FULLCYC=1
|Entry 4.8 (CHK-SPI-FULLCYC), CPHA=0 and CPHA=1
|Same, at x2 and x4
|===

*Open items*. These are not graded, each for the reason given:

* The reset value of STATUS.BYTEORDER. The goldens use `ByteOrder = 1`: the SEP SPI host is the unmodified OpenTitan IP and its register map and semantics are authoritative (`doc/trm/src/revision.adoc`, revision 0.5.34), and the OpenTitan parameter defaults to 1 (`vendor/lowRISC/opentitan/upstream/hw/ip/spi_host/data/spi_host.hjson`, `ByteOrder`: "If ByteOrder = 1, a Little-Endian ordering is used"). `spi_controller.adoc` (STATUS.BYTEORDER) gives the read-only field a reset of 0x0, which does not match; that document line is open (<<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>, SPI byte order inside a word). A `STATUS.BYTEORDER` readback is never the golden.
* Data lane output enables of an x1 RX-only segment and of a dummy segment. No source names them: OpenTitan "SPI Protocol Basics" says that in standard mode SD[0] is always an output, and OpenTitan "Command Interface" says that in a dummy segment neither side transmits. Entry 4.7 logs them (`OBS-SPI-OE-X1RX`, `OBS-SPI-OE-DUMMY`) and grades them not.
* The words that the RX path holds past RXFULL, and CMDQD reaching 4. No source states them: `theory_of_operation.md:780` names "the RX FIFO plus any intervening buffers" and gives no count, and `spi_host.hjson:250-252` ties READY to no queue depth. Entry 4.9 logs them (`OBS-SPI-RX-EXTRA`) and grades the TX depth of 73 words, the RX depth of 64 words and the CMDQD bound of 4 (CHK-SPI-DEPTH-TX, CHK-SPI-DEPTH-RX, CHK-SPI-DEPTH-CMD; `spi_host.hjson:93-107`, `sep-programming.adoc:784`).
* The shift edge at CPHA=1 with FULLCYC=1: `spi_host.hjson:378-379`, `theory_of_operation.md:42` and `theory_of_operation.md:298` state different edges. Entry 4.8 logs the cell (`OBS-SPI-CPHA`) and grades the FULLCYC sampling point at CPHA=1. Entry 4.8 grades CSNLEAD, CSNTRAIL and CSNIDLE as the minimum bounds the OpenTitan theory of operation states and logs the margin (OBS-SPI-CSN-EXACT; at CPHA=1 the trail is one half sck cycle above its minimum).
* Code coverage. This statement covers functional checks only.

This statement lists what the tests grade. It does not claim that every combination of width, mode, length and timing is graded.

'''''
